    <div class="comment-body markdown-body js-comment-body soft-wrap user-select-contain d-block">
      <h1 dir="auto"><strong>Offline-first, SQLite-backed library</strong> ready for production.</h1>
<p dir="auto">Repo and the superpowers documentation here: [<a href="https://github.com/equationalapplications/expo-llm-wiki">equationalapplications/expo-llm-wiki</a>](<a href="https://github.com/equationalapplications/expo-llm-wiki">https://github.com/equationalapplications/expo-llm-wiki</a>)</p>
<p dir="auto">Most RAG implementations treat every "chunk" of data as equal. The result? Your LLM gets "context pollution"—distracted by a random observation from three days ago while ignoring your core system instructions.</p>
<p dir="auto">Inspired by Andrej Karpathy's LLM Wiki spec, the <strong><code class="notranslate">expo-llm-wiki</code></strong> monorepo introduces a <strong>Tiered Memory Architecture</strong>. This allows you to give your AI a structured "brain" using cross-entity namespaces and configurable weights.</p>
<hr>
<h2 dir="auto">The "Brain" Hierarchy</h2>
<p dir="auto">The <strong>LLM Librarian</strong> manages a knowledge hierarchy that mimics human expertise:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>The Fact Tier (Immutable Truth)</strong></li>
</ol>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>What it is:</strong> Static documents (specs, PDFs).</li>
<li><strong>The Role:</strong> The highest source of truth; if the Librarian finds a contradiction, the <strong>Fact</strong> always wins.</li>
<li><strong>The Benefit:</strong> Immutable so hard truths never get diluted.</li>
</ul>
<ol start="2" dir="auto">
<li><strong>The Working Memory Tier (The Context)</strong></li>
</ol>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>What it is:</strong> The active project environment (codebase or work-in-progress).</li>
<li><strong>The Role:</strong> Real-time episodic events and observations.</li>
<li><strong>The Benefit:</strong> Uses <strong>recency weighting</strong> to stay aligned with the "now."</li>
</ul>
<ol start="3" dir="auto">
<li><strong>The Wisdom Tier (The Evolving Wiki)</strong></li>
</ol>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>What it is:</strong> A synthesized repository where the Librarian "remembers" lessons and patterns.</li>
<li><strong>The Role:</strong> Consolidates Working Memory into long-term architectural or stylistic preferences.</li>
<li><strong>The Benefit:</strong> Uses <strong>accessCount weighting</strong> so frequently referenced "lessons" graduate into Core Wisdom.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<h2 dir="auto">Production-Grade Superpowers</h2>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Hybrid Retrieval Engine:</strong> Uses <strong>Cosine Similarity</strong> for semantic search when online, with an automatic fallback to <strong>MiniSearch</strong> for full-text search when the device is offline.</li>
<li><strong>The Pipeline:</strong> Uses <code class="notranslate">runLibrarian()</code> to consolidate episodic events into durable facts and <code class="notranslate">runHeal()</code> to resolve contradictions and prune stale claims.</li>
<li><strong>Multi-Entity Architecture:</strong> Support for thousands of isolated users/agents within a single SQLite database using <code class="notranslate">entityId</code> namespaces—zero memory leakage.</li>
<li><strong>React &amp; Mobile Native Optimized:</strong> Includes reactive hooks (<code class="notranslate">useMemoryRead</code>) and "Emoji-Safe" chunking to prevent common mobile LLM UI bugs.</li>
<li><strong>Security &amp; GDPR:</strong> Built-in <code class="notranslate">runPrune</code> and <code class="notranslate">forget</code> methods for "Right to be Forgotten" compliance, plus source normalization to prevent path injection.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<h2 dir="auto">Implementation Example:</h2>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts" dir="auto"><pre class="notranslate"><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">bundle</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">wiki</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">read</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">'facts'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">'wip_codebase'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">'wisdom_cache'</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">'Synthesize current state.'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">systemPrompt</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">formatContext</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">bundle</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">factWeights</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">confidence</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1.5</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>  <span class="pl-c">// Prioritize immutable Facts</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">recency</span>: <span class="pl-c1">0.9</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>     <span class="pl-c">// Keep Working Memory relevant</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">accessCount</span>: <span class="pl-c1">0.5</span>  <span class="pl-c">// Surface Wisdom that the user relies on most</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Tech Stack:</strong> Expo, React Native, SQLite, MiniSearch.</p>
    </div>

</div>
